3.3
Admissible Manifold

Definition 3.9
.

For every
\tilde{x}\in RST
, an
s
-admissible manifold

V^{s}
at double chart
\psi_{\tilde{x}}^{p^{s},p^{u}}
has the form of

V^{s}=\psi_{\tilde{x}}\big\{(v_{1},G(v_{1})):v_{1}\in B^{d_{s}(\tilde{x})}_{p^{s}}(0)\big\},

where
B^{d_{s}(\tilde{x})}_{p^{s}}(0)\subset\mathbb{R}^{d_{s}(\tilde{x})}
is a ball at the origin with radius
p^{s}
and
G:B^{d_{s}(\tilde{x})}_{p^{s}}(0)\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{d_{u}(\tilde{x})}
is a
C^{1+\frac{\beta}{2}}
function such that

(AM1)

|G(0)|\leq 10^{-3}(p^{s}\land p^{u})^{2}
;

(AM2)

\|d_{0}G\|\leq\frac{1}{2}(p^{s}\land p^{u})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}
;

(AM3)

\|d_{\cdot}G\|_{\frac{\beta}{2}}=\|d_{\cdot}G\|_{C^{0}}+H\ddot{o}l_{\frac{\beta}{2}}(d_{\cdot}G)<\frac{1}{2}
.

A
u
-admissible manifold

V^{u}
at double chart
\psi_{\tilde{x}}^{p^{s},p^{u}}
has the form of

V^{u}=\psi_{\tilde{x}}\big\{(G(v_{2}),v_{2}):v_{2}\in B^{d_{u}(\tilde{x})}_{p^{u}}(0)\big\},

where
G:B^{d_{u}(\tilde{x})}_{p^{u}}(0)\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{d_{s}(\tilde{x})}
is a
C^{1+\frac{\beta}{2}}
function such that
(AM1)
-
(AM3)
. The map
G
is called the
representing function
.

By assumptions
(A1)
-
(A3)
, the representing function
G
satisfies:
\forall t\in B_{p^{s/u}}(0)\subset\mathbb{R}^{d_{s/u}(\tilde{x})}

\|d_{t}G\|\leq\|d_{0}G\|+H\ddot{o}l_{\frac{\beta}{2}}(d_{\cdot}G)|t|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\leq\frac{1}{2}(p^{s}\land p^{u})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+\frac{1}{2}(p^{s/u})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\leq(p^{s/u})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\leq\epsilon\implies Lip(G)\leq\epsilon,

(3.14)

and

|G(t)|\leq|G(0)|+Lip(G)\cdot p^{s/u}\leq 10^{-3}(p^{s}\land p^{u})^{2}+\epsilon p^{s/u}\leq 10^{-2}p^{s/u}.

(3.15)

Let
v:=\psi_{\tilde{x}}^{p^{s},p^{u}}
, denote
\mathcal{M}^{s}(v):=\{V^{s}:V^{s}\text{ is the $s$-admissible manifold at $v$\lx@text@rbrace}
, endowing the metric
d_{C^{0}}(V_{1},V_{2}):=\|G_{1}-G_{2}\|_{C^{0}}
and
d_{C^{1}}(V_{1},V_{2}):=\|d_{\cdot}G_{1}-d_{\cdot}G_{2}\|_{C^{0}}
on
B_{p^{s}}(0)
, where
G_{1},G_{2}
are the representing functions of
V_{1},V_{2}
, respectively. Similarly, we can define
\mathcal{M}^{u}(v)
.

Proposition 3.11
.

For
\epsilon>0
small enough, let
v:=\psi_{\tilde{x}}^{p^{s},p^{u}}
, then for every
V^{s}\in\mathcal{M}^{s}(v)
and
V^{u}\in\mathcal{M}^{u}(v)
, we have

(1)

\exists!\ p=\psi_{\tilde{x}}(v_{1})
s.t.
V^{s}\bigcap V^{u}=\{p\}
, where
v_{1}\in\mathbb{R}^{d},|v|\leq 10^{-2}(p^{s}\land p^{u})^{2}
;

(2)

Define
P:\mathcal{M}^{s}(v)\times\mathcal{M}^{u}(v)\rightarrow M
by
(V^{s},V^{u})\mapsto p
, where
p
is the intersection of
V^{s}
and
V^{u}
. Then
P
is a Lipschitz map with Lipschitz constant 3 i.e. if
V^{s}_{i}\bigcap V^{u}_{i}=\{p_{i}\}
for
i=1,2
, then
d(p_{1},p_{2})\leq 3[d_{C^{0}}(V^{s}_{1},V^{s}_{2})+d_{C^{0}}(V^{u}_{1},V^{u}_{2})]
, where
V_{i}^{s}\in\mathcal{M}^{s}(v),V_{i}^{u}\in\mathcal{M}^{u}(v)
.

Proof 8
.

Since the process of proof does not involve whether the mapping
f
is invertible, this proof is the same as Ovadia’s
[
13
]
.

\Box

The following theorem will show that the Graph Transform preserves admissibility.

Theorem 3.12
.

For
\epsilon>0
small enough, given two double charts
w_{1}=\psi_{\tilde{x}}^{p^{s},p^{u}},w_{2}=\psi_{\tilde{y}}^{q^{s},q^{u}}
, and let
V^{s}
be a
s
-admissible manifold in
w_{2}
. If
w_{1}\rightarrow w_{2}
, then

(1)

f_{\tilde{x}}^{-1}V^{s}
contains a unique
s
-admissible manifold in
\psi_{\tilde{x}}^{p^{s},p^{u}}
, denote the
s
-admissible manifold as
\mathcal{F}^{s}_{w_{1},w_{2}}(V^{s})
;

(2)

For any
u
-admissible
V^{u}
in
\psi_{\tilde{x}}^{p^{s},p^{u}}
,
f_{\tilde{x}}^{-1}V^{s}
intersects
V^{u}
at a unique point;

(3)

If
p:=\psi_{\tilde{y}}(0,G(0))\in V^{s}
, then
f^{-1}_{\tilde{x}}(p)\in\mathcal{F}^{s}_{w_{1},w_{2}}(V^{s})
.

For the
u
-case, there are similar statements.

Proof 9
.

Suppose
G
is the representing function of
V^{s}
, then
V^{s}=\psi_{\tilde{y}}\{(v,G(v)):v\in B_{q^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{y})}(0)\}
. Notice that
\psi_{\tilde{x}}^{-1}(f_{\tilde{x}}^{-1}(V^{s}))=\psi_{\tilde{x}}^{-1}(f_{\tilde{x}}^{-1}(\psi_{\tilde{y}}\text{Graph}(G)))=F^{-1}_{\tilde{x},\tilde{y}}\text{Graph}(G)
. Denote
\eta:=q^{s}\land q^{u}
, by Theorem
3.6
,
F^{-1}_{\tilde{x},\tilde{y}}\text{Graph}(G)
can be represented as the form of

\displaystyle\big\{(D_{s}^{-1}(v)+h_{s}^{-}(v,G(v)),D_{u}^{-1}(G(v))+h_{u}^{-}(v,G(v))):v\in B_{q^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{y})}(0)\big\},

(3.16)

where
\|D_{s}\|\leq e^{-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}}
,
\|D_{u}^{-1}\|\leq e^{-\frac{1}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}}
,
|h_{s/u}^{-}(0,0)|\leq\epsilon\eta^{3}
,
\|dh_{s/u}^{-}\|\leq\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}
, and
H\ddot{o}l_{\frac{\beta}{2}}\bar{H}^{-}=H\ddot{o}l_{\frac{\beta}{2}}(h_{s}^{-},h_{u}^{-})\leq\sqrt{\epsilon}
.

(1) Using graph transform, we will prove that (
3.16
) can be represented as the form of
\{(w,F(w)):w\in B_{p^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{x})}(0)\}
and
F
satisfies
(AM1)
-
(AM3)
. As the ideas in
[
9
,
16
]
, denote
\phi(v):=D_{s}^{-1}(v)+h_{s}^{-}(v,G(v))
on
B_{q^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{y})}(0)
, then
F=[D_{u}^{-1}(G(\cdot))+h_{u}^{-}(\cdot,G(\cdot)))]\circ\phi^{-1}
. The map
\phi^{-1}
is well-defined on
\phi(B_{q^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{y})}(0))
since
\phi
is expanding on
B_{q^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{y})}(0)
. Indeed,
\forall v\in B_{q^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{y})}(0)

\displaystyle\|d_{v}\phi\|

\displaystyle=\|D_{s}^{-1}+d_{(v,G(v))}h_{s}^{-}\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{v}G\end{pmatrix}\|\geq\|D_{s}^{-1}\|-\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}(\|d_{v}G\|+1)

\displaystyle\geq\|D_{s}^{-1}\|-\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}(\epsilon+1)\text{ (by inequality (\ref{G1}))}

\displaystyle\geq\|D_{s}^{-1}\|\big(1-\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}(\epsilon+1)\big)\text{ (since }\|D_{s}^{-1}\|\geq\|D_{s}\|^{-1}\geq e^{\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}}\geq 1)

\displaystyle\geq e^{-\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}}\|D_{s}\|^{-1}\ (\epsilon\text{ is small enough s.t. }\sqrt{\epsilon}(\epsilon+1)\ll 1),

where
I
is a
d_{s}(\tilde{y})\times d_{s}(\tilde{y})
identity matrix. By Lemma
3.2
(1)(3),
\|C_{0}(\tilde{y})^{-1}\|^{2}\geq s^{2}(\tilde{y})
and

\|C_{0}(\tilde{y})^{-1}\|\geq\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\|C_{0}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))^{-1}\|d(p(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}),\Gamma_{\infty})^{2a}\geq\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\|C_{0}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))^{-1}\|\rho(\tilde{y})^{2a}.

And so

e^{-\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}}\|D_{s}\|^{-1}\geq e^{-Q(\tilde{y})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}}\cdot e^{\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}}\geq e^{-Q(\tilde{y})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+\|C_{0}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))^{-1}\|^{-2}}\geq e^{-Q(\tilde{y})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\rho(\tilde{y})^{4a}\|C_{0}(\tilde{y})^{-1}\|^{-2}}>1,

(3.17)

since
Q(\tilde{y})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\leq(\epsilon^{\frac{20}{\beta}})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\|C_{0}(\tilde{y})^{-1}\|^{-2}\rho(\tilde{y})^{4a}\leq\frac{1}{2}\rho(\tilde{y})^{4a}\|C_{0}(\tilde{y})^{-1}\|^{-2}
(recall that
\gamma\geq\frac{20}{\beta}
and
a>1
).

Claim 1
.

For small enough
\epsilon
, the image
\phi(B_{q^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{y})}(0))
contains
B_{e^{\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}-\frac{1}{2}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}}q^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{y})}(0)
, then
\phi^{-1}
is well defined on
B_{e^{\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}-\frac{1}{2}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}}q^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{y})}(0)
and it satisfies

(a)

\|d_{\cdot}\phi^{-1}\|<e^{\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}}
;

(b)

|\phi^{-1}(0)|<2\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{2+\frac{\beta}{2}}
;

(c)

\|d_{\cdot}\phi^{-1}\|_{\frac{\beta}{2}}\leq e^{6\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}}
.

Proof 10
.

For
v\in B_{q^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{y})}(0)
,
d_{v}\phi=D_{s}^{-1}+d_{(v,G(v))}h_{s}^{-}\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{v}G\end{pmatrix}=:D_{s}^{-1}+A(v)
. Estimate for
\|A(v)\|
:
\forall v_{1},v_{2}\in B_{q^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{y})}(0)

\displaystyle\|A(v_{1})-A(v_{2})\|=\Big\|d_{(v_{1},G(v_{1}))}h_{s}^{-}\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{v_{1}}G\end{pmatrix}-d_{(v_{2},G(v_{2}))}h_{s}^{-}\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{v_{2}}G\end{pmatrix}\Big\|

(3.18)

\displaystyle\leq\Big\|d_{(v_{1},G(v_{1}))}h_{s}^{-}\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{v_{1}}G\end{pmatrix}-d_{(v_{1},G(v_{1}))}h_{s}^{-}\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{v_{2}}G\end{pmatrix}\Big\|+\Big\|d_{(v_{1},G(v_{1}))}h_{s}^{-}\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{v_{2}}G\end{pmatrix}-d_{(v_{2},G(v_{2}))}h_{s}^{-}\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{v_{2}}G\end{pmatrix}\Big\|

\displaystyle\leq\|d_{(v_{1},G(v_{1}))}h_{s}^{-}\|\cdot\|d_{v_{1}}G-d_{v_{2}}G\|+\|d_{(v_{1},G(v_{1}))}h_{s}^{-}-d_{(v_{2},G(v_{2}))}h_{s}^{-}\|\cdot\Big\|\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{v_{2}}G\end{pmatrix}\Big\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\cdot\frac{1}{2}|v_{1}-v_{2}|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+\sqrt{\epsilon}|v_{1}-v_{2}|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\cdot(1+\epsilon)^{1+\frac{\beta}{2}}\leq 3\sqrt{\epsilon}|v_{1}-v_{2}|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}.

Hence,
H\ddot{o}l_{\frac{\beta}{2}}A\leq 3\sqrt{\epsilon}
. For
v\in B_{q^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{y})}(0)
,

\|A(v)\|\leq\|A(0)\|+H\ddot{o}l_{\frac{\beta}{2}}A\cdot|v|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\leq\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}(1+\epsilon)+3\sqrt{\epsilon}(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\leq 5\sqrt{\epsilon}(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}.

(3.19)

Estimate for the error term
h_{s}^{-}
:
\forall v\in B_{q^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{y})}(0)
,

|h_{s}^{-}(v,G(v))|\leq|h_{s}^{-}(0,0)|+\|d_{\cdot}h_{s}^{-}\|\cdot(|v|+|G(v)|)\leq\epsilon\eta^{3}+2\sqrt{\epsilon}(q^{s})^{1+\frac{\beta}{2}}\leq 3\sqrt{\epsilon}(q^{s})^{1+\frac{\beta}{2}},

by inequality (
3.15
).
Given
u\in B_{e^{\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}-\frac{1}{2}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}}q^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{y})}(0)
, assume
\phi(v)=u
. Define the map
T
on
B_{q^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{x})}(0)
by

T(v)=D_{s}[u-h_{s}^{-}(v,G(v))].

Then
\phi(v)=u
iff
v
is the fixed point of
T
. Next, we will prove that
T
has a unique fixed point
v_{0}
and
v_{0}\in B_{q^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{y})}(0)
. Indeed,

\displaystyle|T(v)|

\displaystyle\leq\|D_{s}\|(|\phi(v)|+|h_{s}^{-}(v,G(v))|)

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}}(e^{\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}-\frac{1}{2}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}}q^{s}+3\sqrt{\epsilon}(q^{s})^{1+\frac{\beta}{2}})

\displaystyle\leq(e^{-\frac{1}{2}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}}+3\sqrt{\epsilon}(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}})q^{s}\leq q^{s}.

This implies that
T(B_{q^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{y})}(0))\subset B_{q^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{y})}(0)
. And
\|d_{v}T\|\leq\|D_{s}\|\cdot\|A(v)\|\leq e^{\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}}\cdot 4\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\leq 1
. By the fixed point theorem,
T
has the unique fixed point
v_{0}\in B_{q^{s}}(0)
.

(a)
\|d_{\cdot}\phi^{-1}\|=\|(d_{\cdot}\phi)^{-1}\|\leq\|(d_{\cdot}\phi)\|^{-1}\leq e^{\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}}\|D_{s}\|<e^{\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}}
.

(b) Since
|\phi(0)|=|h_{s}^{-}(0,G(0))|\leq|h_{s}^{-}(0,0)|+\|d_{\cdot}h_{s}^{-}\|\cdot|G(0)|\leq\epsilon\eta^{3}+\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\cdot 10^{-3}\eta^{2}\leq 2\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{2+\frac{\beta}{2}}
, then
\phi(0)\in B_{e^{\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}-\frac{1}{2}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}}q^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{x})}(0)
for small enough
\epsilon
. Hence,

|\phi^{-1}(0)|=|\phi^{-1}(0)-\phi^{-1}(\phi(0))|\leq\|d(\phi^{-1})\|\cdot|\phi(0)|<e^{\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}}\cdot 2\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{2+\frac{\beta}{2}}<2\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{2+\frac{\beta}{2}}.

(c) Let
w:=\phi(v)
, then
d_{w}\phi^{-1}=(D_{s}^{-1}+A(v))^{-1}=(Id+D_{s}A(v))^{-1}D_{s}
. Hence

\displaystyle\|d_{\cdot}\phi^{-1}\|_{\frac{\beta}{2}}\leq\|(Id+D_{s}A(\phi^{-1}(\cdot)))^{-1}\|_{\frac{\beta}{2}}\cdot\|D_{s}\|_{\frac{\beta}{2}}\leq\frac{1}{1-\|D_{s}A(\phi^{-1}(\cdot))\|_{\frac{\beta}{2}}}\cdot\|D_{s}\|_{\frac{\beta}{2}}.

(3.20)

Notice that
D_{s}
and
\phi^{-1}
are contractive since
\|d(\phi^{-1})\|<e^{\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}}<1
. By inequalities (
3.18
) and (
3.19
),
\|A(v)\|_{\frac{\beta}{2}}\leq 5\sqrt{\epsilon}(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+3\sqrt{\epsilon}\leq 8\sqrt{\epsilon}
, and
\|D_{s}A(\phi^{-1}(\cdot))\|_{\frac{\beta}{2}}\leq e^{-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}}\cdot 8\sqrt{\epsilon}<1
. Hence,

\|d_{\cdot}\phi^{-1}\|_{\frac{\beta}{2}}\leq\frac{1}{1-8\sqrt{\epsilon}}\cdot e^{-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}}\leq e^{10\sqrt{\epsilon}-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}},

for small enough
\epsilon>0
.

\Box

Claim 2
.

The map
F:\mathbb{R}^{d_{s}(\tilde{x})}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{d_{u}(\tilde{x})}
satisfies
(AM1)
-
(AM3)
.

Proof 11
.

(AM1)
: Since
Lip(G)<\epsilon
and
|G(0)|\leq 10^{-3}(p^{s}\land p^{u})^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle|(\phi^{-1}(0),G(\phi^{-1}(0)))|

\displaystyle\leq|\phi^{-1}(0)|+|G(0)|+Lip(G)\cdot|\phi^{-1}(0)|

(3.21)

\displaystyle\leq(1+\epsilon)2\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{2+\frac{\beta}{2}}+10^{-3}\eta^{2}\leq 2\epsilon^{2}\eta^{2}.

Then

\displaystyle|F(0)|

\displaystyle=|D_{u}^{-1}(G(\phi^{-1}(0)))+h_{u}^{-}(\phi^{-1}(0),G(\phi^{-1}(0)))|

\displaystyle\leq\|D_{u}^{-1}\|(|G(0)|+Lip(G)|(\phi^{-1}(0)|)+(|h_{u}^{-}(0)|+\|d_{\cdot}h_{u}^{-}\|\cdot|(\phi^{-1}(0),G(\phi^{-1}(0)))|)

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\frac{1}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}}(10^{-3}\eta^{2}+\epsilon\cdot 2\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{2+\frac{\beta}{2}})+\epsilon\eta^{3}+\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\cdot 2\epsilon^{2}\eta^{2}\text{ (by inequality (\ref{phi1})})

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\frac{1}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}}[10^{-3}\eta^{2}+\epsilon\cdot 2\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{2+\frac{\beta}{2}}+\sqrt{2}d(p(\tilde{y}),\Gamma_{\infty})^{-a}\cdot 2\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{2+\frac{\beta}{2}}]\text{ (by Lemma \ref{C}}(2))

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\frac{1}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}}\cdot 10^{-3}\eta^{2}[1+2\cdot 10^{3}(\epsilon+\sqrt{2}d(p(\tilde{y}),\Gamma_{\infty})^{-a})\cdot\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}]

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\frac{1}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}}\cdot 10^{-3}(p^{s}\land p^{u})^{2}\cdot e^{\epsilon\eta^{\frac{\beta}{4}}}(\frac{q^{s}\land q^{u}}{p^{s}\land p^{u}})^{2}\text{ (since }\eta^{\frac{\beta}{4}}\cdot d(p(\tilde{y}),\Gamma_{\infty})^{-a})\ll\epsilon)

\displaystyle\leq 10^{-3}(p^{s}\land p^{u})^{2}\cdot e^{-\frac{1}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}}\cdot e^{\epsilon\eta^{\frac{\beta}{4}}}\cdot e^{2\Gamma\eta^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}}\text{ (since }p^{s}\land p^{u}\geq I^{-1}(q^{s}\land q^{u}))

\displaystyle\leq 10^{-3}(p^{s}\land p^{u})^{2}\cdot e^{-\frac{1}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}+3\Gamma\eta^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}}\text{ (since }\frac{1}{\gamma}<\frac{\beta}{20}<\frac{\beta}{4}).

And
-\frac{1}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}+3\Gamma\eta^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}\leq-\frac{1}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}+3\Gamma Q(\tilde{y})^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}\leq-\frac{1}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}+\|C(\tilde{y})^{-1}\|^{-2}<0
, then
|F(0)|\leq 10^{-3}(p^{s}\land p^{u})^{2}
.

(AM2)
: According to
\|d_{\cdot}G\|_{\frac{\beta}{2}}=\|d_{\cdot}G\|_{C^{0}}+H\ddot{o}l_{\frac{\beta}{2}}(d_{\cdot}G)<\frac{1}{2}
and claim
1
(a)
,

\|d_{\phi^{-1}(0)}G\|\leq\|d_{0}G\|+H\ddot{o}l_{\frac{\beta}{2}}d_{\cdot}G\cdot|\phi^{-1}(0)|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\leq\|d_{0}G\|+\frac{1}{2}\cdot(2\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{2+\frac{\beta}{2}})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\leq\|d_{0}G\|+\epsilon^{2}\eta^{\beta}(<\epsilon).

(3.22)

By the proof of Theorem
3.6
and inequality (
3.21
),

\|d_{(\phi^{-1}(0),G(\phi^{-1}(0))}h_{u}^{-}\|\leq\sqrt{\epsilon}|(\phi^{-1}(0),G(\phi^{-1}(0))|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\leq\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\beta}.

(3.23)

Next, we estimate
\|d_{0}F\|
:

\displaystyle\|d_{0}F\|

\displaystyle=\Big\|D_{u}^{-1}\cdot d_{\phi^{-1}(0)}G\cdot d_{0}\phi^{-1}+d_{(\phi^{-1}(0),G(\phi^{-1}(0))}h_{u}^{-}\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{\phi^{-1}(0)}G\end{pmatrix}d_{0}\phi^{-1}\Big\|

\displaystyle\leq\big[\|D_{u}^{-1}\|\cdot\|d_{\phi^{-1}(0)}G\|+\|d_{(\phi^{-1}(0),G(\phi^{-1}(0))}h_{u}^{-}\|\cdot(1+\|d_{\phi^{-1}(0)}G\|)\big]\cdot\|d_{0}\phi^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq\big[\|D_{u}^{-1}\|\cdot(\|d_{0}G\|+\epsilon^{2}\eta^{\beta})+\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\beta}\cdot 2\big]\cdot e^{(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}}\text{ (by inequalities (\ref{dF1}), (\ref{dF2}), claim \ref{phi}\ref{a}})

\displaystyle\leq e^{(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}-\frac{1}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}}\cdot(\|d_{0}G\|+\epsilon^{2}\eta^{\beta}+2\sqrt{2}d(p(\tilde{y}),\Gamma_{\infty})^{-a}\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\beta})\text{ (by Lemma \ref{C}(2)})

\displaystyle\leq e^{(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}-\frac{1}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}}\cdot\frac{1}{2}(p^{s}\land p^{u})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\Big[\frac{\|d_{0}G\|}{\frac{1}{2}(p^{s}\land p^{u})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}}+(\frac{q^{s}\land q^{u}}{p^{s}\land p^{u}})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\epsilon\eta^{\frac{\beta}{4}}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq e^{(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}-\frac{1}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}}\cdot\frac{1}{2}(p^{s}\land p^{u})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\cdot e^{\frac{\beta}{2}\Gamma(p^{s}\land p^{u})^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}}(1+\epsilon\eta^{\frac{\beta}{4}})\text{ (by }q^{s}\land q^{u}\leq I(p^{s}\land p^{u}))

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}(p^{s}\land p^{u})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\cdot e^{(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}-\frac{1}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}+\frac{\beta}{2}\Gamma(p^{s}\land p^{u})^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}+\epsilon\eta^{\frac{\beta}{4}}}.

Similar to the calculation of inequality (
3.17
), there exists a constant
k_{0}=k_{0}(\Gamma,\beta,\epsilon)
s.t.

\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}\geq k_{0}Q(\tilde{y})^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}\geq k_{0}Q(\tilde{y})^{\frac{\beta}{2}},

(3.24)

and

\frac{1}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}\geq k_{0}Q(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}\geq k_{0}Q(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))^{\frac{\beta}{2}}.

(3.25)

Then
(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}-\frac{1}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}+\frac{\beta}{2}\Gamma(p^{s}\land p^{u})^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}+\epsilon\eta^{\frac{\beta}{4}}\leq Q(\tilde{y})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}-\frac{1}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}+\frac{\beta}{2}\Gamma e^{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}+Q(\tilde{y})^{\frac{\beta}{4}}<0.

Hence,
\|d_{0}F\|\leq\frac{1}{2}(p^{s}\land p^{u})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}
.

(AM3)
: By Theorem
3.6
,
(1+\epsilon)q^{s}+10^{-3}\eta^{2}\leq 2q^{s}
. And
\|d_{\cdot}h_{u}^{-}\|_{\frac{\beta}{2}}\leq\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+\sqrt{\epsilon}\leq 3\sqrt{\epsilon}(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}
. Then

\displaystyle\|d_{\cdot}F\|_{\frac{\beta}{2}}

\displaystyle=\|D_{u}^{-1}\cdot d_{\phi^{-1}(\cdot)}G\cdot d_{\cdot}\phi^{-1}+d_{(\phi^{-1}(\cdot),G(\phi^{-1}(\cdot))}h_{u}^{-}\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{\phi^{-1}(\cdot)}G\end{pmatrix}d_{\cdot}\phi^{-1}\|_{\frac{\beta}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\big[\|D_{u}^{-1}\|\cdot\|d_{\cdot}G\|_{\frac{\beta}{2}}+\|d_{\cdot}h_{u}^{-}\|_{\frac{\beta}{2}}(1+\|d_{\cdot}G\|_{\frac{\beta}{2}})\big]\|d_{\cdot}\phi^{-1}\|_{\frac{\beta}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq e^{6\sqrt{\epsilon}(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}}(\|d_{\cdot}G\|_{\frac{\beta}{2}}\|D_{u}^{-1}\|+4\sqrt{\epsilon})

\displaystyle\leq e^{6\sqrt{\epsilon}(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}-\frac{1}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}}(\|d_{\cdot}G\|_{\frac{\beta}{2}}+4e^{\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{\epsilon})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}e^{6\sqrt{\epsilon}(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}-\frac{1}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}}\cdot e^{8e^{\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{\epsilon}}.

And by inequality (
3.24
),
6\sqrt{\epsilon}(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}-\frac{1}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}+8e^{\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{\epsilon}<0
, then
\|d_{\cdot}F\|_{\frac{\beta}{2}}<\frac{1}{2}
.

\Box

Since
\frac{1}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}>\|C(\tilde{y})^{-1}\|^{-2}\geq(\frac{1}{2}+\Gamma)(q^{s})^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}\geq\frac{1}{2}(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+\Gamma(q^{s})^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}
and
p^{s}=\min\{I(q^{s}),Q(\tilde{x})\}
, for
v\in B_{q^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{y})}(0)
,

e^{\frac{1}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}-\frac{1}{2}(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}}q^{s}\geq e^{\Gamma(q^{s})^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}}q^{s}=I(q^{s})\geq p^{s},

(3.26)

for small enough
\epsilon
. Hence,
f^{-1}_{\tilde{x}}V^{s}
contains an
s
-admissible manifold
\mathcal{F}^{s}_{w_{1},w_{2}}(V^{s})=\psi_{\tilde{x}}\{(w,F(w)):w\in B_{p^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{x})}(0)\}
. The proof for the uniqueness of the
s
-admissible manifold contained in
f_{\tilde{x}}^{-1}V^{s}
is the same as the proof in
[
13
]
.

(2) For any
u
-admissible
V^{u}
in
\psi_{\tilde{x}}^{p^{s},p^{u}}
,
f_{\tilde{x}}^{-1}V^{s}
intersects
V^{u}
at least at one point by Proposition
3.11
(1). And by McShane’s extension formula, we can prove
f_{\tilde{x}}^{-1}V^{s}
intersects
V^{u}
at most at one point. The detailed proof is shown in
[
13
]
.

(3) By the Theorem
3.6
and
|G(0)|\leq 10^{-3}(p^{s}\land p^{u})^{2}
, we have

|h_{s}^{-}(0,G(0))|\leq|h_{s}^{-}(0,0)|+\|d_{\cdot}h\|\cdot|G(0)|\leq\epsilon\eta^{3}+\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\cdot 10^{-3}\eta^{2}\leq\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{2+\frac{\beta}{2}}\leq\sqrt{\epsilon}(I(p^{s}\land p^{u}))^{2+\frac{\beta}{2}}\ll p^{s}.

\Box

Remark 4
.

Since the map
f
has singularities, there is no uniform lower bound on
\|D_{s}\|
and
\|D_{u}^{-1}\|
. By Lemma
3.2
(2), we have
\|D_{s}\|,\|D_{u}^{-1}\|\geq\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}d(x,\Gamma_{\infty})^{a}
. Hence, stronger control over the origin offset
|G(0)|
is required when proving
F
satisfies the admissibility parameters
(AM1)
-
(AM3)
.

Definition 3.10
.

Suppose
v_{1}\rightarrow v_{2}
, where
v_{1}=\psi_{\tilde{x}}^{p^{s},p^{u}},v_{2}=\psi_{\tilde{y}}^{q^{s},q^{u}}
.

(1)

The
Graph Transform

\mathcal{F}^{s}_{v_{1},v_{2}}
maps an
s
-admissible manifold
V^{s}
in
\psi_{\tilde{y}}^{q^{s},q^{u}}
with representing function
F:B_{q^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{y})}(0)\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{d_{u}(\tilde{y})}
to the unique
s
-admissible manifold in
\psi_{\tilde{x}}^{p^{s},p^{u}}
with representing function
\tilde{F}:B_{p^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{x})}(0)\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{d_{u}(\tilde{x})}
such that it is contained in
f^{-1}_{\tilde{x}}(V^{s})
and

\{(v,\tilde{F}(v)):v\in B_{p^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{x})}(0)\}\subset F^{-1}_{\tilde{x},\tilde{y}}\{(v,F(v)):v\in B^{d_{s}(\tilde{y})}_{q^{s}}(0)\}.

(2)

The
Graph Transform

\mathcal{F}^{u}_{v_{1},v_{2}}
maps a
u
-admissible manifold
V^{u}
in
\psi_{\tilde{x}}^{p^{s},p^{u}}
with representing function
G:B_{p^{u}}^{d_{u}(\tilde{x})}(0)\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{d_{s}(\tilde{x})}
to the unique
u
-admissible manifold in
\psi_{\tilde{y}}^{q^{s},q^{u}}
with representing function
\tilde{G}:B_{q^{u}}^{d_{u}(\tilde{y})}(0)\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{d_{s}(\tilde{y})}
such that it is contained in
f(V^{u})
and

\{(\tilde{G}(v),v):v\in B_{q^{u}}^{d_{u}(\tilde{y})}(0)\}\subset F_{\tilde{x},\tilde{y}}\{(G(v),v):v\in B^{d_{u}(\tilde{x})}_{p^{u}}(0)\}.

Proposition 3.13
.

Suppose
w_{1}\rightarrow w_{2}
, where
w_{1}=\psi_{\tilde{x}}^{p^{s},p^{u}},w_{2}=\psi_{\tilde{y}}^{q^{s},q^{u}}
. For small enough
\epsilon
, and
s
-admissible manifolds
V_{1}^{s},V_{2}^{s}
in
\psi_{\tilde{y}}^{q^{s},q^{u}}
with the representing functions
F_{1},F_{2}
, respectively:

(1)

d_{C^{0}}(\mathcal{F}^{s}_{w_{1},w_{2}}(V_{1}^{s}),\mathcal{F}^{s}_{w_{1},w_{2}}(V_{1}^{s}))\leq e^{-\frac{1-\epsilon}{u^{2}(\tilde{y}))}}d_{C^{0}}(V_{1}^{s},V_{2}^{s})
.

(2)

d_{C^{1}}(\mathcal{F}^{s}_{w_{1},w_{2}}(V_{1}^{s}),\mathcal{F}^{s}_{w_{1},w_{2}}(V_{1}^{s}))\leq e^{-\frac{1-\epsilon}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}-\frac{1-\epsilon}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}}\big((1+\epsilon(q^{s}\land q^{u})^{\frac{\beta}{2}})\|d_{\cdot}F_{1}-d_{\cdot}F_{2}\|+10\epsilon\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\big)
.

For
u
-admissible manifolds
V_{1}^{u},V_{2}^{u}
in
\psi_{\tilde{x}}^{p^{s},p^{u}}
with the representing functions
G_{1},G_{2}
, respectively:

(3)

d_{C^{0}}(\mathcal{F}^{u}_{w_{1},w_{2}}(V_{1}^{u}),\mathcal{F}^{u}_{w_{1},w_{2}}(V_{1}^{u}))\leq e^{-\frac{1-\epsilon}{s^{2}(\tilde{x}))}}d_{C^{0}}(V_{1}^{u},V_{2}^{u})
.

(4)

d_{C^{1}}(\mathcal{F}^{u}_{w_{1},w_{2}}(V_{1}^{u}),\mathcal{F}^{u}_{w_{1},w_{2}}(V_{1}^{u}))\leq e^{-\frac{1-\epsilon}{s^{2}(\tilde{x}))}-\frac{1-\epsilon}{u^{2}(\hat{f}(\tilde{x}))}}\big((1+\epsilon(p^{s}\land p^{u})^{\frac{\beta}{2}})\|d_{\cdot}G_{1}-d_{\cdot}G_{2}\|+10\epsilon\|G_{1}-G_{2}\|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\big)
.

Proof 12
.

By Theorem
3.12
,
\mathcal{F}^{s}_{w_{1},w_{2}}(V_{1}^{s})=\psi_{\tilde{x}}[\{(v,\tilde{F_{i}}(v):v\in B_{p^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{x})}(0)\}]
, where
\tilde{F}_{i}=[D_{u}^{-1}(F_{i}(\cdot))+h_{u}^{-}(\cdot,F_{i}(\cdot)))]\circ\phi_{i}^{-1}
,
\phi_{i}(v):=D_{s}^{-1}(v)+h_{s}^{-}(v,F_{i}(v))
on
B_{q^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{y})}(0)
.

(1)

\displaystyle\|\phi_{1}^{-1}-\phi_{2}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\phi_{1}^{-1}\circ\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}^{-1}\circ\phi_{1}\|=\|Id-\phi_{2}^{-1}\circ\phi_{1}\|

(3.27)

\displaystyle=\|\phi_{2}^{-1}\circ\phi_{2}-\phi_{2}^{-1}\circ\phi_{1}\|\leq\|d_{\cdot}\phi_{2}^{-1}\|\cdot\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|d_{\cdot}\phi_{2}^{-1}\|\cdot\|h_{s}^{-}(\cdot,F_{1}(\cdot))-h_{s}^{-}(\cdot,F_{2}(\cdot))\|

\displaystyle\leq\|d_{\cdot}\phi_{2}^{-1}\|\cdot\|d_{\cdot}h_{s}^{-}\|\cdot\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|,

the first inequality holds by
\phi_{i}(B_{q^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{y})}(0))\supset B_{p^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{x})}(0)
. Then
\frac{\|\phi_{1}^{-1}-\phi_{2}^{-1}\|}{\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|}\leq\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}
since
\|d_{\cdot}\phi_{2}^{-1}\|\leq 1
(by claim
1
(a)
,
\eta=q^{s}\land q^{u}
). And

\displaystyle\|F_{1}\circ\phi_{1}^{-1}-F_{2}\circ\phi_{2}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|F_{1}\circ\phi_{1}^{-1}-F_{1}\circ\phi_{2}^{-1}\|+\|F_{1}\circ\phi_{2}^{-1}-F_{2}\circ\phi_{2}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq Lip(F_{1})\|\phi_{1}^{-1}-\phi_{2}^{-1}\|+\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|,

since
\phi_{i}
is expanding on
B_{q^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{y})}(0)
, then
\frac{\|F_{1}\circ\phi_{1}^{-1}-F_{2}\circ\phi_{2}^{-1}\|}{\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|}\leq 1+\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}
by inequality (
3.14
). Hence, we can estimate

\displaystyle d_{C^{0}}(\mathcal{F}^{s}_{w_{1},w_{2}}(V_{1}^{s}),^{s}(V^{s}_{2}))

\displaystyle=\|[D_{u}^{-1}(F_{1}(\cdot))+h_{u}^{-}(\cdot,F_{1}(\cdot)))]\circ\phi_{1}^{-1}-[D_{u}^{-1}(F_{2}(\cdot))+h_{u}^{-}(\cdot,F_{2}(\cdot)))]\circ\phi_{2}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|D_{u}^{-1}\|\cdot\|F_{1}\circ\phi_{1}^{-1}-F_{2}\circ\phi_{2}^{-1}\|+\|d_{\cdot}h_{u}^{-}\|(\|\phi_{1}^{-1}-\phi_{2}^{-1}\|+\|F_{1}\circ\phi_{1}^{-1}-F_{2}\circ\phi_{2}^{-1}\|)

\displaystyle\leq\|D_{u}^{-1}\|[(1+\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}})+\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}(\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+1+\epsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}})]\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\frac{1}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}+2\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}}\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|.

For small enough
\epsilon
,
\frac{\epsilon}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}\geq 2\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}
, thus part(1) is proved.

(2) For
\tilde{v}\in B_{e^{\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}-\frac{1}{2}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}}q^{s}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{y})}(0)
, and let
\tilde{v}=\phi_{1}(v)
:

\displaystyle\|d_{\tilde{v}}\phi_{1}^{-1}-d_{\tilde{v}}\phi_{2}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|Id-d_{\tilde{v}}\phi_{2}^{-1}\cdot d_{v}\phi_{1}\|=\|d_{\tilde{v}}\phi_{2}^{-1}\cdot d_{v}\phi_{2}-d_{\tilde{v}}\phi_{2}^{-1}\cdot d_{v}\phi_{1}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|d_{v}\phi_{1}-d_{v}\phi_{2}\|\cdot\|d_{\tilde{v}}\phi_{2}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq\Big\|d_{(v,F_{1}(v))}h_{s}^{-}\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{v}F_{1}\end{pmatrix}-d_{(v,F_{2}(v))}h_{s}^{-}\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{v}F_{2}\end{pmatrix}\Big\|\cdot\|d_{\tilde{v}}\phi_{2}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq\Big[\Big\|d_{(v,F_{1}(v))}h_{s}^{-}\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{v}F_{1}\end{pmatrix}-d_{(v,F_{1}(v))}h_{s}^{-}\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{v}F_{2}\end{pmatrix}\Big\|

\displaystyle+\Big\|d_{(v,F_{1}(v))}h_{s}^{-}\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{v}F_{2}\end{pmatrix}-d_{(v,F_{2}(v))}h_{s}^{-}\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{v}F_{2}\end{pmatrix}\Big\|\Big]\cdot\|d_{\tilde{v}}\phi_{2}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq[\|d_{\cdot}h_{s}^{-}\|\cdot\|d_{\cdot}F_{1}-d_{\cdot}F_{2}\|+Hol_{\frac{\beta}{2}}(d_{\cdot}h_{s}^{-})\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}(1+\|d_{\cdot}F_{2}\|)]\cdot\|d_{\tilde{v}}\phi_{2}^{-1}\|,

then

\|d_{\tilde{v}}\phi_{1}^{-1}-d_{\tilde{v}}\phi_{2}^{-1}\|\leq e^{(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}}(\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\|d_{\cdot}F_{1}-d_{\cdot}F_{2}\|+2\sqrt{\epsilon}\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}).

(3.28)

Denote
v_{1}:=\phi_{1}^{-1}(\tilde{v})
and
v_{2}:=\phi_{2}^{-1}(\tilde{v})
, we estimate

\displaystyle\quad\|d_{(v_{1},F_{1}(v_{1}))}h_{u}^{-}-d_{(v_{2},F_{2}(v_{2}))}h_{u}^{-}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|d_{(v_{1},F_{1}(v_{1}))}h_{u}^{-}-d_{(v_{1},F_{2}(v_{1}))}h_{u}^{-}\|+\|d_{(v_{1},F_{2}(v_{1}))}h_{u}^{-}-d_{(v_{1},F_{2}(v_{2}))}h_{u}^{-}\|+\|d_{(v_{1},F_{2}(v_{2}))}h_{u}^{-}-d_{(v_{2},F_{2}(v_{2}))}h_{u}^{-}\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\epsilon}\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+\sqrt{\epsilon}|v_{1}-v_{2}|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+\sqrt{\epsilon}|v_{1}-v_{2}|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\leq 3\sqrt{\epsilon}\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|^{\frac{\beta}{2}},

since
|v_{1}-v_{2}|=|\phi_{1}^{-1}(\tilde{v})-\phi_{2}^{-1}(\tilde{v})|\leq\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|
by part (1). Then

\displaystyle\Big\|d_{(v_{1},F_{1}(v_{1}))}h_{u}^{-}\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{v_{1}}F_{1}\end{pmatrix}-d_{(v_{2},F_{2}(v_{2}))}h_{u}^{-}\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{v_{2}}F_{2}\end{pmatrix}\Big\|

\displaystyle\leq\|d_{(v_{1},F_{1}(v_{1}))}h_{u}^{-}-d_{(v_{2},F_{2}(v_{2}))}h_{u}^{-}\|\cdot\Big\|\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{v_{1}}F_{1}\end{pmatrix}\Big\|+\|d_{\cdot}h_{u}^{-}\|\cdot\Big\|\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{v_{1}}F_{1}\end{pmatrix}-\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{v_{2}}F_{2}\end{pmatrix}\Big\|

\displaystyle\leq Hol_{\frac{\beta}{2}}(h_{u}^{-})3\sqrt{\epsilon}\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}(1+\|d_{\cdot}F_{1}\|)+\|d_{\cdot}h_{u}^{-}\|\cdot\|d_{v_{1}}F_{1}-d_{v_{2}}F_{2}\|

\displaystyle\leq 6\epsilon\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}(\|d_{v_{1}}F_{1}-d_{v_{2}}F_{1}\|+\|d_{v_{2}}F_{1}-d_{v_{2}}F_{2}\|)

\displaystyle\leq 6\epsilon\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\Big(\frac{1}{2}|v_{1}-v_{2}|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+\|d_{\cdot}F_{1}-d_{\cdot}F_{2}\|\Big)

\displaystyle\leq 7\epsilon\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\|d_{\cdot}F_{1}-d_{\cdot}F_{2}\|.

It follows that

\displaystyle\Big\|d_{(v_{1},F_{1}(v_{1}))}h_{u}^{-}\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{v_{1}}F_{1}\end{pmatrix}d_{\tilde{v}}\phi_{1}^{-1}-d_{(v_{2},F_{2}(v_{2}))}h_{u}^{-}\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{v_{2}}F_{2}\end{pmatrix}d_{\tilde{v}}\phi_{2}^{-1}\Big\|

\displaystyle\leq\Big\|d_{(v_{1},F_{1}(v_{1}))}h_{u}^{-}\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{v_{1}}F_{1}\end{pmatrix}-d_{(v_{2},F_{2}(v_{2}))}h_{u}^{-}\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{v_{2}}F_{2}\end{pmatrix}\Big\|\cdot\|d_{\tilde{v}}\phi_{1}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle+\Big\|d_{(v_{2},F_{2}(v_{2}))}h_{u}^{-}\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{v_{2}}F_{2}\end{pmatrix}\Big\|\cdot\|d_{\tilde{v}}\phi_{1}^{-1}-d_{\tilde{v}}\phi_{2}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq e^{(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}}(7\epsilon\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\|d_{\cdot}F_{1}-d_{\cdot}F_{2}\|)

\displaystyle+2\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}e^{(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}}(\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\|d_{\cdot}F_{1}-d_{\cdot}F_{2}\|+2\sqrt{\epsilon}\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|^{\frac{\beta}{2}})

\displaystyle\leq e^{(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}}\big[(\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+2\epsilon\eta^{\beta})\|d_{\cdot}F_{1}-d_{\cdot}F_{2}\|+(7\epsilon+4\epsilon\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}})\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\big].

And by claim
1
(a)
and inequality (
3.28
) , we have

\displaystyle\|d_{v_{1}}F_{1}\cdot d_{\tilde{v}}\phi_{1}^{-1}-d_{v_{2}}F_{2}\cdot d_{\tilde{v}}\phi_{2}^{-1}\|\leq\|d_{\cdot}F_{1}-d_{\cdot}F_{2}\|\cdot\|d_{\cdot}\phi_{1}^{-1}\|+\|d_{\cdot}F_{2}\|\cdot\|d_{\cdot}\phi_{1}^{-1}-d_{\cdot}\phi_{2}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq e^{(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}}\|d_{\cdot}F_{1}-d_{\cdot}F_{2}\|+\epsilon e^{(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}}(\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\|d_{\cdot}F_{1}-d_{\cdot}F_{2}\|+2\sqrt{\epsilon}\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|^{\frac{\beta}{2}})

\displaystyle\leq e^{(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}}[(1+\epsilon\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}})\|d_{\cdot}F_{1}-d_{\cdot}F_{2}\|+2\epsilon\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}].

Hence,

\displaystyle\|d_{\tilde{v}}\tilde{F}_{1}-d_{\tilde{v}}\tilde{F}_{2}\|

\displaystyle=\Big\|D_{u}^{-1}\cdot d_{v_{1}}F_{1}\cdot d_{\tilde{v}}\phi_{1}^{-1}+d_{(v_{1},F_{1}(v_{1}))}h_{u}^{-}\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{v_{1}}F_{1}\end{pmatrix}d_{\tilde{v}}\phi_{1}^{-1}

\displaystyle-D_{u}^{-1}\cdot d_{v_{2}}F_{2}\cdot d_{\tilde{v}}\phi_{2}^{-1}-d_{(v_{2},F_{2}(v_{2}))}h_{u}^{-}\begin{pmatrix}I\\
d_{v_{2}}F_{2}\end{pmatrix}d_{\tilde{v}}\phi_{2}^{-1}\Big\|

\displaystyle\leq\|D_{u}^{-1}\|e^{(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}}\big[(1+\epsilon\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}})\|d_{\cdot}F_{1}-d_{\cdot}F_{2}\|+2\epsilon\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\big]

\displaystyle+e^{(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}}\big[(\sqrt{\epsilon}\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}}+2\epsilon\eta^{\beta})\|d_{\cdot}F_{1}-d_{\cdot}F_{2}\|+(7\epsilon+4\epsilon\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}})\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\big]

\displaystyle\leq e^{(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}-\frac{1}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}-\frac{1}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}}\big((1+\epsilon\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}})\|d_{\cdot}F_{1}-d_{\cdot}F_{2}\|+10\epsilon\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\big).

For small enough
\epsilon
,
\frac{\epsilon}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}+\frac{\epsilon}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}\geq 2(q^{s})^{\frac{\beta}{2}}
, thus
\|d_{\cdot}\tilde{F}_{1}-d_{\cdot}\tilde{F}_{2}\|\leq e^{-\frac{1-\epsilon}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}-\frac{1-\epsilon}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}}\big((1+\epsilon\eta^{\frac{\beta}{2}})\|d_{\cdot}F_{1}-d_{\cdot}F_{2}\|+10\epsilon\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\big)
.

Similarly, we can get the
u
-case.

\Box

In this context, we study the orbits which may have
0
Lyapunov exponents, so the coding map may not be
H\ddot{o}lder
continuous when coding points on these orbits. Ovadia introduced
leaf cores
by shrinking the size of the admissible manifold, then the coding map has summable variations about the leaf cores.

Definition 3.11
.

Given a double chart
\psi_{\tilde{x}}^{p^{s},p^{u}}
. The
leaf cores
of
s/u
-admissible manifold
V^{s/u}
is

\hat{V}^{s/u}:=\psi_{\tilde{x}}\circ Graph(F^{s/u})\text{ on }B_{\eta^{2}}^{d_{s/u}(\tilde{x})}(0),

where
\eta:=p^{s}\land p^{u}
, and
F^{s/u}
is the representing function of the
s/u
-admissible manifold
V^{s/u}
.

Notice that
\hat{V}^{s/u}\subset V^{s/u}
if
\hat{V}^{s/u}
is the leaf cores of
V^{s/u}
.

.

w_{1}\rightarrow w_{2}
, where

, and

d_{C^{0}}(\mathcal{F}^{s}_{w_{1},w_{2}}(\hat{V}_{1}^{s}),\mathcal{F}^{s}_{w_{1},w_{2}}(\hat{V}_{1}^{s}))\leq e^{-\frac{1-\epsilon}{u^{2}(\tilde{y}))}}d_{C^{0}}(\hat{V}_{1}^{s},\hat{V}_{2}^{s})
.

(2)

d_{C^{1}}(\mathcal{F}^{s}_{w_{1},w_{2}}(\hat{V}_{1}^{s}),\mathcal{F}^{s}_{w_{1},w_{2}}(\hat{V}_{1}^{s}))\leq e^{-\frac{1-\epsilon}{s^{2}(\hat{f}^{-1}(\tilde{y}))}-\frac{1-\epsilon}{u^{2}(\tilde{y})}}\big((1+\epsilon(q^{s}\land q^{u})^{\beta})\|d_{\cdot}F_{1}-d_{\cdot}F_{2}\|+10\epsilon\|F_{1}-F_{2}\|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\big)

.

(4)

d_{C^{1}}(\mathcal{F}^{u}_{w_{1},w_{2}}(\hat{V}_{1}^{u}),\mathcal{F}^{u}_{w_{1},w_{2}}(\hat{V}_{1}^{u}))\leq e^{-\frac{1-\epsilon}{s^{2}(\tilde{x}))}-\frac{1-\epsilon}{u^{2}(\hat{f}(\tilde{x}))}}\big((1+\epsilon(p^{s}\land p^{u})^{\beta})\|d_{\cdot}G_{1}-d_{\cdot}G_{2}\|+10\epsilon\|G_{1}-G_{2}\|^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\big)
.

Proof 13
.

We restrict the proof of Theorem
3.12
on
B_{\eta^{2}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{x})}(0)
where
\eta=q^{s}\land q^{u}
, as the calculations of inequality (

), we have

. Hence,

\mathcal{F}^{s}_{w_{1},w_{2}}(\hat{V}_{1}^{s})=\psi_{\tilde{x}}[\{(v,\tilde{F_{i}}(v)):v\in B_{(p^{s}\land p^{u})^{2}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{x})}(0)\}],

where
\tilde{F}_{i}=[D_{u}^{-1}(F_{i}(\cdot))+h_{u}^{-}(\cdot,F_{i}(\cdot))]\circ\phi_{i}^{-1}
,
\phi_{i}(v):=D_{s}^{-1}(v)+h_{s}^{-}(v,F_{i}(v))
on
B_{\eta^{2}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{y})}(0)
. Then we get the corollary by restricting the proof of Proposition
3.13
on
B_{\eta^{2}}^{d_{s}(\tilde{x})}(0)
.
